<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The faces of a cube are painted so that any two faces with an edge in common are painted different colours.

What is the smallest number of colours needed? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The faces of a cube are painted so that any two faces with an edge in common are painted different colours.

What is the smallest number of colours needed? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Key fact</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Opposite faces of a cube do not have an edge in common, so opposite faces can be the same colour.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Face pairs</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Opposite face pairs} &= 3 \\\\
\text{Colours needed}     &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Key fact"&gt;

Opposite faces of a cube do not have an edge in common, so opposite faces can be the same colour.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Face pairs"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Opposite face pairs} &= 3 \\\\
\text{Colours needed}     &= 3
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;3&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A solid wooden cube is painted blue on the outside.

The cube is then cut into eight smaller cubes of equal size.

What is the the total number of faces of the smaller cubes that have blue paint on them?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The large cube is cut into $8$ equal smaller cubes.

Each smaller cube is a corner cube with $3$ outside faces painted blue.

<img src="/assets/papers/missing/missing_image.svg" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="working" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />

</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

                           &= 24
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Chi is facing North East.

In which direction will he be facing if he turns clockwise through $3$ right angles?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Chi is facing North East.

In which direction will he be facing if he turns clockwise through &#36;3&#36; right angles?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using angles measured anticlockwise from East:

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Calculation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{North East direction} &= 45 \degree \\\\
\text{Turn clockwise}       &= 3 \times 90 \degree \\\\
                            &= 270 \degree \\\\
\text{Current direction}    &= 45 \degree - 270 \degree \\\\
                            &= -225 \degree \\\\
                            &= 135 \degree \\\\
                            &= \text{North West}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Current direction $= \text{North West}$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{North West}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Using angles measured anticlockwise from East:

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Calculation"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{North East direction} &= 45 \degree \\\\
\text{Turn clockwise}       &= 3 \times 90 \degree \\\\
                            &= 270 \degree \\\\
\text{Current direction}    &= 45 \degree - 270 \degree \\\\
                            &= -225 \degree \\\\
                            &= 135 \degree \\\\
                            &= \text{North West}
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Current direction &#36;= \text{North West}&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;\text{North West}&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A group of $30$ adults and $16$ children paid  $\pounds 408$ in total to 
watch a football match. Each child ticket cost  $\pounds 3$.

What was the cost of each adult ticket?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A group of &#36;30&#36; adults and &#36;16&#36; children paid  &#36;\pounds 408&#36; in total to 
watch a football match. Each child ticket cost  &#36;\pounds 3&#36;.

What was the cost of each adult ticket?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the cost of an adult ticket (in $\pounds$) $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
30 \times a + 16 \times 3   &= 408 \\\\
30a + 48                    &= 408 \\\\
30a                         &= 408 - 48 \\\\
30a                         &= 360 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {360} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 30} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {30}} {\cancel {30}} \\\\
a                           &= \pounds 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Cost of an adult ticket $= \pounds 12$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 12$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Equation"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
30 \times a + 16 \times 3   &= 408 \\\\

a                           &= \dfrac {360} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times 30} {30} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {30}} {\cancel {30}} \\\\
a                           &= \pounds 12
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Cost of an adult ticket &#36;= \pounds 12&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a sale, prices are reduced by $15 \%$.

What is the sale price of a hoody that originally cost  $\pounds 30?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a sale, prices are reduced by &#36;15 \%&#36;.

What is the sale price of a hoody that originally cost  &#36;\pounds 30?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

If the price is reduced by $15 \%$, then the sale price will be $100 - 15 = 85 \%$ of the original price.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Sale price</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Sale price}      &= 85 \% \ \text{of} \ 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{85}{100} \times 30 \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{85 \times 30}{100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{2550}{100} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 25.50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 25.50$

</div>
</div>

\end{aligned}

</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Guy has twice as many stickers as Asif and five more than Kayal.

They have $35$ stickers altogether.

How many stickers does Guy have?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Guy has twice as many stickers as Asif and five more than Kayal.

They have &#36;35&#36; stickers altogether.

How many stickers does Guy have?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of stickers Asif has $= a$

Number of stickers Guy has $= 2a$

Number of stickers Kayal has $= 2a - 5$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + 2a + 2a - 5             &= 35 \\\\
5a                          &= 35 + 5 \\\\
5a                          &= 40 \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {40}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {5 \times 8}{5} \\\\
a                           &= \dfrac {\cancel{5} \times 8}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a                           &= 8
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Stickers Guy has}     &= 2a \\\\
                            &= 2 \times 8 \\\\
                            &= 16
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$16$

</div>
</div>

The volume of the solid shape is $875 \ \text{cm}^3$.

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the volume of one of the cubes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the volume of one of the cubes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Number of cubes</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

A cube has $6$ faces, so the solid is made from $1 + 6 = 7$ cubes.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Volume</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of 1 cube} &= \dfrac{875}{7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{125 \times 7}{7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{125 \times \cancel{7}}{\cancel{7}} \\\\
                        &= 125 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$125 \ \text{cm}^3$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Number of cubes"&gt;

A cube has &#36;6&#36; faces, so the solid is made from &#36;1 + 6 = 7&#36; cubes.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Volume"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Volume of 1 cube} &= \dfrac{875}{7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{125 \times 7}{7} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac{125 \times \cancel{7}}{\cancel{7}} \\\\
                        &= 125 \ \text{cm}^3
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the length of one side of a cube?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the length of one side of a cube?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">

ABBR: $\text{Volume of a cube} = (\text{Side}) ^ { 3 }$

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Side length} &= \sqrt[3]{125} \\\\
                   &= \sqrt[3]{5 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
                   &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$5 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;

                   &= \sqrt[3]{5 \times 5 \times 5} \\\\
                   &= 5 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The card is **grey** on the front and **black** on the back.

When piece $A$ is turned over, which one of the shapes below shows its **black** side?

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2018/paper-1/questions/s01-q23-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2018/paper-1/questions/s01-q23-i01.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows the coordinates of three points $A, B$ and $C$.

Shape $ABCD$ is a kite.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2018/paper-1/questions/s01-q25-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

What are the coordinated of point $D$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows the coordinates of three points &#36;A, B&#36; and &#36;C&#36;.

**Working**

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>

<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Points $A$ and $C$ both have $y$-coordinate $4$.

So $AC$ is the horizontal line of symmetry.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find x</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Point $D$ is the reflection of point $B$ across $AC$.

A reflection across a horizontal line keeps the $x$-coordinate the same.

So the $x$-coordinate of $D$ is $5$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Find y</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Point $B$ has $y$-coordinate $6$.

This is $2$ above the line of symmetry, so point $D$ is $2$ below it.

So the $y$-coordinate of $D$ is $4 - 2 = 2$.

So $D = (5, 2)$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$(5, 2)$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Line of symmetry"&gt;

Points &#36;A&#36; and &#36;C&#36; both have &#36;y&#36;-coordinate &#36;4&#36;.

So &#36;AC&#36; is the horizontal line of symmetry.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find x"&gt;

Point &#36;D&#36; is the reflection of point &#36;B&#36; across &#36;AC&#36;.

A reflection across a horizontal line keeps the &#36;x&#36;-coordinate the same.

So the &#36;x&#36;-coordinate of &#36;D&#36; is &#36;5&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Find y"&gt;

Point &#36;B&#36; has &#36;y&#36;-coordinate &#36;6&#36;.

This is &#36;2&#36; above the line of symmetry, so point &#36;D&#36; is &#36;2&#36; below it.

So the &#36;y&#36;-coordinate of &#36;D&#36; is &#36;4 - 2 = 2&#36;.

So &#36;D = (5, 2)&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

*ABBR: TODO:: Find the right katex symbol.*

Given that,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{triangle} + \text{triangle}                 &= \text{square} \\\\
\text{square} + \text{triangle}                 &= \text{circle} \\\\
\text{diamond}                 &= \text{circle} + \text{square} + \text{triangle} \\\\
\spadesuit + \spadesuit                 &= \spadesuit \\\\
\spadesuit + \spadesuit                 &= \spadesuit \\\\
\spadesuit + \spadesuit + \spadesuit    &= \spadesuit 
\end{aligned}
$

how many $\spadesuit$ are equal to $\spadesuit$?

how many $\text{triangle}$ are equal to $\text{diamond}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_katex_show_correct_symbol
Given that,

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{triangle} + \text{triangle}                 &= \text{square} \\\\
\text{square} + \text{triangle}                 &= \text{circle} \\\\
\text{diamond}                 &= \text{circle} + \text{square} + \text{triangle} \\\\
\spadesuit + \spadesuit                 &= \spadesuit \\\\
\spadesuit + \spadesuit                 &= \spadesuit \\\\
\spadesuit + \spadesuit + \spadesuit    &= \spadesuit 
\end{aligned}
&#36;

how many &#36;\spadesuit&#36; are equal to &#36;\spadesuit&#36;?

how many &#36;\text{triangle}&#36; are equal to &#36;\text{diamond}&#36;?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the value of triangle $= t$

Value of square $= s$

Value of circle $= c$

Value of diamond $= d$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="equations" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equations</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
2t              &= s & (1) \\\\
s + t           &= c & (2) \\\\
d               &= c + s + t & (3)
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solution</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Substitute equations $(1)$ and $(2)$ into equation $(3)$.

$
\begin{aligned}
d               &= c + s + t \\\\
d               &= (s + t) + (2t) + t \\\\
d               &= (2t + t) + (2t) + t \\\\
d               &= 6t
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of triangles equal to one diamond $= 6$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

Let the value of triangle &#36;= t&#36;

Value of square &#36;= s&#36;

Value of circle &#36;= c&#36;

Value of diamond &#36;= d&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="equations" title="Equations"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
2t              &= s & (1) \\\\
s + t           &= c & (2) \\\\
d               &= c + s + t & (3)
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Solution"&gt;

Substitute equations &#36;(1)&#36; and &#36;(2)&#36; into equation &#36;(3)&#36;.

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
d               &= c + s + t \\\\
d               &= (s + t) + (2t) + t \\\\
d               &= (2t + t) + (2t) + t \\\\
d               &= 6t
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Number of triangles equal to one diamond &#36;= 6&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;6&#36;

<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$5$ toffees weigh the same as $6$ maltesers and $3$ toffees.

How many maltesers would weigh the same as $9$ toffees?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;5&#36; toffees weigh the same as &#36;6&#36; maltesers and &#36;3&#36; toffees.

How many maltesers would weigh the same as &#36;9&#36; toffees?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{toffees}                    &:& \text{maltesers} \\\\
= & (5 - 3)                           &:& 6 \\\\
= & 2                                 &:& 6 \\\\
= & 1                                 &:& \dfrac {6} {2} \\\\
= & 1 \times 9                        &:& \dfrac {6 \times 9} {2} \\\\
= & 9                                 &:& \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 9} {2} \\\\
= & 9                                 &:& \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times 3 \times 9} {\cancel 2} \\\\
= & 9                                 &:& 3 \times 9 \\\\
= & 9                                 &:& 27
\end{array}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of maltesers $= 27$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Alternate solution

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let number of maltesers $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Solve the ratio</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
(5 - 3) \ \text{toffees} : 6 \ \text{maltesers} &= 9 \ \text{toffees} : a \ \text{maltesers} \\\\
\dfrac{2} {6}                                   &= \dfrac {9} {a} \\\\
2 \times a                                      &= 9 \times 6 \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {9 \times 6} {2} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {9 \times 2 \times 3} {2} \\\\
a                                               &= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel 2 \times 3} {\cancel 2} \\\\
a                                               &= 9 \times 3 \\\\
a                                               &= 27
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of maltesers $= 27$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a game you need to get three coloured discs (green, blue and res) in the correct order.

Three attempts have been made and are shown below, with the number of discs that are in the correct positions.

What is the correct order for the three coloured discs?


|  POSITION 1  |  POSITION 2  |  POSITION 3  |  POSITION 4  |
|------------|------------|------------|------------|
|     Red      |     Blue     |    Green     |      One     |
|     Red      |     Green    |     Blue     |     None     |
|     Green    |     Blue     |      Red     |     None     |



</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a game you need to get three coloured discs (green, blue and res) in the correct order.

Three attempts have been made and are shown below, with the number of discs that are in the correct positions.

What is the correct order for the three coloured discs?

&lt;PaperTable&gt;

|  POSITION 1  |  POSITION 2  |  POSITION 3  |  POSITION 4  |
|------------|------------|------------|------------|
|     Red      |     Blue     |    Green     |      One     |
|     Red      |     Green    |     Blue     |     None     |
|     Green    |     Blue     |      Red     |     None     |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Red</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The second row has no correct positions, so Red is not in position $1$.

The third row has no correct positions, so Red is not in position $3$.

Therefore, Red must be in position $2$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Green</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The second row has no correct positions, so Green is not in position $2$.

The third row has no correct positions, so Green is not in position $1$.

Therefore, Green must be in position $3$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Order</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Red is in position $2$ and Green is in position $3$, so Blue must be in position $1$.

The correct order is Blue, Red, Green.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Blue, Red, Green

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Red"&gt;

The second row has no correct positions, so Red is not in position &#36;1&#36;.

The third row has no correct positions, so Red is not in position &#36;3&#36;.

Therefore, Red must be in position &#36;2&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Green"&gt;

The second row has no correct positions, so Green is not in position &#36;2&#36;.

The third row has no correct positions, so Green is not in position &#36;1&#36;.

Therefore, Green must be in position &#36;3&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Order"&gt;

Red is in position &#36;2&#36; and Green is in position &#36;3&#36;, so Blue must be in position &#36;1&#36;.

The correct order is Blue, Red, Green.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

$16808 \div 382$

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 16808 \div 382 \\\\
&= \dfrac {16808} {382} \\\\
&= \dfrac {44 \times 382} {382} \\\\

&= 44
\end{aligned}
$

**Question**

$1680.8 \div 44$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

\begin{aligned}

&= \dfrac{\cancel{44} \times 382}{10 \times \cancel{44}}  \\\\
&= \dfrac{382}{10} \\\\
&= 38.2
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The diagram shows a pentagonal prism.

Write the letter of the one shape below that is a net for the pentagonal prism.

<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2018/paper-1/questions/s01-q30-i00.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>
<div class="paper-image-layout" data-indent="none">
<img src="/assets/papers/bancrofts-school/2018/paper-1/questions/s01-q30-i01.png" alt="" data-slot="paper-image" data-asset-scope="question" data-alt-review="pending" data-kind="essential" data-display-size="sm" data-align="center" data-indent="none" class="paper-image" style="width: 100%; max-width: 16rem; margin: 1rem auto; height: auto; display: block;" />
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The diagram shows a pentagonal prism.

Write the letter of the one shape below that is a net for the pentagonal prism.

&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
&lt;PaperImage assetScope="question" kind="essential" /&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Net faces</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

A pentagonal prism needs $5$ rectangular faces and $2$ pentagonal faces.

In the net, the $5$ rectangles must fold around the pentagon to make the side faces.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check options</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Shape A has only $4$ rectangles in the strip, so one side face is missing.

Shape B has the right number of faces, but both pentagons are attached on the same side of the rectangle strip.

Shape C does not have $2$ pentagonal ends.

Shape D has $5$ rectangles around one pentagon, and the other pentagon attached to an outside rectangle.

Shape E does not put the $5$ rectangles around one pentagon cleanly.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The correct net is D.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

D

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Net faces"&gt;

A pentagonal prism needs &#36;5&#36; rectangular faces and &#36;2&#36; pentagonal faces.

In the net, the &#36;5&#36; rectangles must fold around the pentagon to make the side faces.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Check options"&gt;

Shape A has only &#36;4&#36; rectangles in the strip, so one side face is missing.

Shape B has the right number of faces, but both pentagons are attached on the same side of the rectangle strip.

Shape C does not have &#36;2&#36; pentagonal ends.

Shape D has &#36;5&#36; rectangles around one pentagon, and the other pentagon attached to an outside rectangle.

Shape E does not put the &#36;5&#36; rectangles around one pentagon cleanly.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

The correct net is D.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

# Section D
<ul class="question decimal question_type_question list_type_decimal">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_ng3 rag_prns question" id="question-bancrofts-school-11-plus-maths-2018-paper-1-2-1">
<div class="uuid">
<p>90AB10AD-2DC4-4437-A8A1-15ADD1B592E6</p>
</div>
<div class="question-nav">

<p>bancrofts-school--11-plus--maths--2018--paper-1:2:1</p>

</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_ng3</p>
</div>
<div class="rag">
<p>rag_wf_prns</p>
</div>
<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">
<ul style="display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; justify-content: flex-start; gap: 0.55rem; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none;">
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
frame.algebra
</span>
</li>
<li style="margin: 0; padding: 0;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #9f1239; border-radius: 999px; background: #fce7f3; color: #881337; padding: 0.38rem 1rem; font-size: 1.2rem; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 0.02em;">
marker.use-trial-error
</span>
</li>
</ul>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Dave has a lot of tables.

Each rectangular table seats $8$ people.

Each round table seats $5$ people.

What is the smallest number of tables of each type that 
Dave needs to use to seat $35$ friends and himself, without any empty seats? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Dave has a lot of tables.

Each rectangular table seats &#36;8&#36; people.

Each round table seats &#36;5&#36; people.

What is the smallest number of tables of each type that 
Dave needs to use to seat &#36;35&#36; friends and himself, without any empty seats? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of rectangular tables $= r$

Let the number of round tables $= c$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
8r + 5c &= 35 + 1 \\\\
         &= 36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check the possible values</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.


Test whole-number values of $r$.


| $r$   | Seats left $= 36 - 8r$ | Divisible by $5$? | $c$ |
|-----|----------------------|-----------------|---|
| $1$   | $36 - 8 \times 1 = 28$ | No  | - |
| $2$   | $36 - 8 \times 2 = 20$ | Yes | $20 \div 5 = 4$ |
| $3$   | $36 - 8 \times 3 = 12$ | No  | - |
| $4$   | $36 - 8 \times 4 = 4$  | No  | - |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of rectangular tables $= 2$

Number of round tables $= 4$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

**Answer**

rectangular tables $=$ $2$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

round tables $=$ $4$

<div class="workingscodeblock">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

\end{aligned}
&#36;

rtq_abbr_working_note_md_trial_and_error_find_answer

| &#36;2&#36;   | &#36;36 - 8 \times 2 = 20&#36; | Yes | &#36;20 \div 5 = 4&#36; |
| &#36;3&#36;   | &#36;36 - 8 \times 3 = 12&#36; | No  | - |
| &#36;4&#36;   | &#36;36 - 8 \times 4 = 4&#36;  | No  | - |

&lt;/PaperTable&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Which one of the following numbers is three **less** than a multiple of $5$ and three **more** than a multiple of $6?$

- $12$
- $17$
- $21$
- $22$
- $27$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which one of the following numbers is three **less** than a multiple of &#36;5&#36; and three **more** than a multiple of &#36;6?&#36;

- &#36;12&#36;
- &#36;17&#36;
- &#36;21&#36;
- &#36;22&#36;
- &#36;27&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Conditions</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.


A number that is $3$ less than a multiple of $5$ becomes a multiple of $5$ when $3$ is added.

A number that is $3$ more than a multiple of $6$ becomes a multiple of $6$ when $3$ is subtracted.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check options</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">


| Number | Add $3$ | Subtract $3$ | Works? |
|------|-------|------------|------|
| $12$ | $15$ | $9$ | No |
| $17$ | $20$ | $14$ | No |
| $21$ | $24$ | $18$ | No |
| $22$ | $25$ | $19$ | No |
| $27$ | $30$ | $24$ | Yes |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The only number that satisfies both conditions is $27$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Conditions"&gt;

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many odd three-digits numbers is it possible to make, using just the numbers $7, 8$ and $9?$

(You are allowed to use each of the numbers more than once in a particular three-digit number.)

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many odd three-digits numbers is it possible to make, using just the numbers &#36;7, 8&#36; and &#36;9?&#36;

(You are allowed to use each of the numbers more than once in a particular three-digit number.)
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">List the numbers</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The possible odd numbers are:

- Ends in $7$: $777, 787, 797, 877, 887, 897, 977, 987, 997$
- Ends in $9$: $779, 789, 799, 879, 889, 899, 979, 989, 999$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Count from the list</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of odd numbers $= 9 + 9 = 18$

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Repetition is allowed, so the hundreds and tens places each have $3$ choices.

The units digit must be odd, so it can only be $7$ or $9$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Using permutation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Hundreds place} &= 3 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Tens place}     &= 3 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Units place}    &= 2 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Total numbers}  &= 3 \times 3 \times 2 \\\\
                      &= 18
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of odd three-digit numbers $= 18$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$18$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

- Ends in &#36;7&#36;: &#36;777, 787, 797, 877, 887, 897, 977, 987, 997&#36;
- Ends in &#36;9&#36;: &#36;779, 789, 799, 879, 889, 899, 979, 989, 999&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Count from the list"&gt;

Number of odd numbers &#36;= 9 + 9 = 18&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

The units digit must be odd, so it can only be &#36;7&#36; or &#36;9&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Using permutation"&gt;

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
\text{Hundreds place} &= 3 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Tens place}     &= 3 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Units place}    &= 2 \text{ choices} \\\\
\text{Total numbers}  &= 3 \times 3 \times 2 \\\\
                      &= 18
\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

Number of odd three-digit numbers &#36;= 18&#36;

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Fill in each box with either $+$ or $-$ to make the answer of $100$.

$
123 \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \
45  \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \ 
67  \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \ 
89  \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \ = \ 100
$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Fill in each box with either &#36;+&#36; or &#36;-&#36; to make the answer of &#36;100&#36;.

&#36;
123 \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \
45  \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \ 
67  \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \ 
89  \ \boxed{\ \phantom{+} \ } \ = \ 100
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: Using BIDMAS

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
123 \ \boxedFilledValue{-} \
45  \ \boxedFilledValue{-} \ 
67  \ \boxedFilledValue{+} \ 
89 = \ 100
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$123 - 45 - 67 + 89 = 100$

</div>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

</span>
</li>

<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">
<span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; border: 1px solid #15803d; border-radius: 999px; background: #dcfce7; color: #14532d; padding: 0.24rem 0.7rem; font-size: 0.95rem; font-weight: 700;">
inherits parent tags: yes
</span>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Last year, $275$ of the $500$ pupils at Woodgreen School were boys.

This year, there are $540$ pupils in the school but the proportion of boys is the same as last year.

How many boys are at the school this year?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Last year, &#36;275&#36; of the &#36;500&#36; pupils at Woodgreen School were boys.

This year, there are &#36;540&#36; pupils in the school but the proportion of boys is the same as last year.

How many boys are at the school this year?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the number of boys this year $= a$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a} {540}                    &= \dfrac {275} {500} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {540 \times 275} {500} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {27 \times 20 \times 25 \times 11} {25 \times 20} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {27 \times \cancel {20} \times \cancel {25} \times 11} {\cancel {25} \times \cancel {20}} \\\\
a                                   &= 27 \times 11 \\\\
a                                   &= 297
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Number of boys this year $= 297$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$297$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup"&gt;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

\end{aligned}
&#36;

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="topics" style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: 0.85rem 0 1rem;">

frame.algebra

<div style="display: flex; justify-content: flex-start; margin: -0.4rem 0 1rem;">

inherits parent tags: yes
</span>
