<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Maya cuts a $6 \ \text{metre}$ piece of string unto three different pieces.

The longest piece is $40 \ \text{cm}$ longer than the middle piece.

The middle piece is $70 \ \text{cm}$ longer than the shorter piece.

How long (in centimetres) is the longest piece?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Maya cuts a &#36;6 \ \text{metre}&#36; piece of string unto three different pieces.

The longest piece is &#36;40 \ \text{cm}&#36; longer than the middle piece.

The middle piece is &#36;70 \ \text{cm}&#36; longer than the shorter piece.

How long (in centimetres) is the longest piece?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required.

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Let the longest piece length $= a \ \text{cm}$

Length of middle piece $= (a - 40) \ \text{cm}$

Length of shortest piece $= (a - 40 - 70) \ \text{cm}$

Total length $= 6 \ \text{m} = 600 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Equation</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
a + (a - 40) + (a - 40 - 70) &= 600 \\\\
a + (a - 40) + (a - 110)     &= 600 \\\\
a + a - 40 + a - 110         &= 600 \\\\
3a - 150                     &= 600 \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {600 + 150} {3} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {750} {3} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {3 \times 250} {3} \\\\
a                            &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 250} {\cancel 3} \\\\
a                                                                &= 250 \ \text{cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Longest piece length $= 250 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$250 \ \text{cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

For example, $2 * 7 = (5 \times 2) + 7 = 17$.

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the value of $9 * 8$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the value of &#36;9 * 8&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 9 * 8 \\\\
&= (5 \times 9) + 8 \\\\
&= 45 + 8 \\\\
&= 53
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$53$

</div>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{aligned}
&= 9 * 8 \\\\
&= (5 \times 9) + 8 \\\\
&= 45 + 8 \\\\
&= 53
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What number does $m$ stand for, if $m * 6 = 41?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What number does &#36;m&#36; stand for, if &#36;m * 6 = 41?&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
m * 6                   &= 41 \\\\
(5 \times m) + 6        &= 41 \\\\
5m                      &= 41 - 6 \\\\
m                       &= \dfrac {35} {5} \\\\
m                       &= \dfrac {5 \times 7} {5} \\\\
m                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 5 \times 7} {\cancel 5} \\\\
m                       &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If $n * 4 = 8 * n$, what number does $n$ stand for?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If &#36;n * 4 = 8 * n&#36;, what number does &#36;n&#36; stand for?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

From the question,


$
\begin{aligned}
n * 4                   &= 8 * n \\\\
(5 \times n) + 4        &= (5 \times 8) + n \\\\
5n + 4                  &= 40 + n \\\\
5n - n                  &= 40 - 4 \\\\
4n                      &= 36 \\\\
n                       &= \dfrac {36} {4} \\\\
n                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 9} {4} \\\\
n                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 9} {\cancel 4} \\\\
n                       &= 9
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_md_opener_question

&#36;
\begin{aligned}
n * 4                   &= 8 * n \\\\
(5 \times n) + 4        &= (5 \times 8) + n \\\\
5n + 4                  &= 40 + n \\\\
5n - n                  &= 40 - 4 \\\\
4n                      &= 36 \\\\
n                       &= \dfrac {36} {4} \\\\
n                       &= \dfrac {4 \times 9} {4} \\\\
n                       &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 9} {\cancel 4} \\\\
n                       &= 9
\end{aligned}
&#36;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Rob, Bertie and Dom ran a race.

Just one of the following four statements is true.

Which one is it?

-  Rob came $1 ^ \text{st}$.
-  Bertie came $1 ^ \text{st}$.
-  Dom came $2 ^ \text{nd}$.
-  Dom did not come $2 ^ \text{nd}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Rob, Bertie and Dom ran a race.

Just one of the following four statements is true.

Which one is it?

-  Rob came &#36;1 ^ \text{st}&#36;.
-  Bertie came &#36;1 ^ \text{st}&#36;.
-  Dom came &#36;2 ^ \text{nd}&#36;.
-  Dom did not come &#36;2 ^ \text{nd}&#36;.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="solve" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check the options</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

- Options $\text{C}$ and $\text{D}$ are opposites, so exactly one of them must be true.
- Since just one statement is true, options $\text{A}$ and $\text{B}$ must be false.
- If options $\text{A}$ and $\text{B}$ are false, then Rob and Bertie did not come $1^\text{st}$. Therefore, Dom came $1^\text{st}$.
- Option $\text{C}$ says that Dom came $2^\text{nd}$, so option $\text{C}$ is false.
- Therefore, option $\text{D}$ is true.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The true statement is option $\text{D}$: Dom did not come $2^\text{nd}$.

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Dom did not come $2^\text{nd}$.

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="solve" title="Check the options"&gt;

- Options &#36;\text{C}&#36; and &#36;\text{D}&#36; are opposites, so exactly one of them must be true.
- Since just one statement is true, options &#36;\text{A}&#36; and &#36;\text{B}&#36; must be false.
- If options &#36;\text{A}&#36; and &#36;\text{B}&#36; are false, then Rob and Bertie did not come &#36;1^\text{st}&#36;. Therefore, Dom came &#36;1^\text{st}&#36;.
- Option &#36;\text{C}&#36; says that Dom came &#36;2^\text{nd}&#36;, so option &#36;\text{C}&#36; is false.
- Therefore, option &#36;\text{D}&#36; is true.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;

&lt;WorkingSection phase="conclusion" title="Answer"&gt;

The true statement is option &#36;\text{D}&#36;: Dom did not come &#36;2^\text{nd}&#36;.

&lt;/WorkingSection&gt;
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

Label the points on the middle line from left to right $B, C, D, E$.

Label the points on the base from left to right $F, G, H, I$.

Designer note: the points should sit on the same four sloping lines, so $A, B, F$ are on one line, $A, C, G$ are on the next line, $A, D, H$ are on the next line, and $A, E, I$ are on the last line.

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Top-part triangles</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The triangles in the top part are:

- $\triangle ABC$
- $\triangle ACD$
- $\triangle ADE$
- $\triangle ABD$
- $\triangle ACE$
- $\triangle ABE$

Count $= 6$

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">

</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

- $\triangle AFG$
- $\triangle AGH$
- $\triangle AHI$
- $\triangle AFH$
- $\triangle AGI$
- $\triangle AFI$

Count $= 6$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

\begin{aligned}
\text{Top-part triangles}    &= 6 \\\\
\text{Full-height triangles} &= 6 \\\\
\text{Total triangles}      &= 6 + 6 \\\\
                            &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$
\begin{array}{cccc}
        &   & B & C \\
{}\times &   & B & C \\
\hline
        & A & B & C
\end{array}
$


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&#36;
\begin{array}{cccc}
        &   & B & C \\
{}\times &   & B & C \\
\hline
        & A & B & C
\end{array}
&#36;

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="setup" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Conditions</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

Since $BC \times BC = ABC$, the square of $BC$ must be a $3$-digit number that ends in the digits $BC$.

The square is $3$ digits, so $BC$ must be between $10$ and $31$.

The units digit comes from $C \times C$.

The non-zero digits whose square ends in the same digit are $1$, $5$ and $6$, so $C$ must be one of these.

So the only values to check are $11$, $15$, $16$, $21$, $25$, $26$ and $31$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check candidates</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.



| $BC$ | $BC \times BC$ | Ends in $BC?$ |
|----|--------------|-------------|
| $11$ | $11 \times 11 = 121$ | No |
| $15$ | $15 \times 15 = 225$ | No |
| $16$ | $16 \times 16 = 256$ | No |
| $21$ | $21 \times 21 = 441$ | No |
| $25$ | $25 \times 25 = 625$ | Yes |
| $26$ | $26 \times 26 = 676$ | No |
| $31$ | $31 \times 31 = 961$ | No |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$A = 6$

$B = 2$

$C = 5$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$A$ $=$ $6$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$B$ $=$ $2$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$C$ $=$ $5$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">&lt;WorkingSection phase="setup" title="Conditions"&gt;

<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Conditions</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

The answer is $EEE$, so it must be one of $111, 222, 333, 444, 555, 666, 777, 888, 999$

Since $DEF + DEF + DEF = EEE$, find $DEF$ by dividing by $3$.

$DEF$ must satisfy both of these conditions:

- It must be a $3$-digit number.
- Its middle digit must match the digit $E$ in $EEE$.

</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="verify" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Check candidates</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

ABBR: We can find the answer using trial and error.



| $EEE$ | $DEF = EEE \div 3$ | Fits $DEF$? |
|-----|------------------|-----------|
| $111$ | $111 \div 3 = 37$ | No, not $3$ digits |
| $222$ | $222 \div 3 = 74$ | No, not $3$ digits |
| $333$ | $333 \div 3 = 111$ | No |
| $444$ | $444 \div 3 = 148$ | Yes |
| $555$ | $555 \div 3 = 185$ | No |
| $666$ | $666 \div 3 = 222$ | No |
| $777$ | $777 \div 3 = 259$ | No |
| $888$ | $888 \div 3 = 296$ | No |
| $999$ | $999 \div 3 = 333$ | No |


</div>
</div>

<div class="paper-working-section" data-phase="conclusion" style="margin: 1rem 0;">
<div class="paper-working-section-header" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
<div style="font-size: 0.78rem; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.04em; text-transform: uppercase; color: #5b6573;">Answer</div>
<div style="flex: 1; height: 1px; background: #d2d8df;"></div>
</div>
<div class="paper-working-section-body">

$D = 1$

$E = 4$

$F = 8$

</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$D$ $=$ $1$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$E$ $=$ $4$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$F$ $=$ $8$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">


<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
